# Proposed OEIS comments: A251328 to A253450

One section per OEIS entry: the date the result was obtained, the paper that proves it, what the entry still records as unsettled, and the text proposed for posting on that entry.

**Nothing here has been posted.** See [../SUBMITTING.md](../SUBMITTING.md).

---

## A251328

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A251328>  
Result obtained: **31 August 2026**  
Paper: [8680](../papers/8501-9000/8680-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical: a(n) = 8*a(n-1) - 13*a(n-2) - 12*a(n-3).
> Conjectures from _Colin Barker_, Nov 29 2018: (Start)

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The empirical recurrence is true. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 9 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that; checking the one above is then a finite exact computation, and it holds for n > 2. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

---


## A251332

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A251332>  
Result obtained: **2 September 2026**  
Paper: [186](../papers/0001-0500/0186-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical recurrence of order 77 (see link above).

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The recurrence in the linked file is correct, and it is the one below. Counting the arrays a row at a time gives a transfer matrix with 729 states, so a(n) satisfies a constant-coefficient linear recurrence of order at most that, and Berlekamp-Massey on exact terms returns the minimal one. Its order is 77, the order stated here, and so is the order of every tail. Any recurrence of that order the sequence satisfies therefore has the same characteristic polynomial, so it is this one. - _Adrian Perez Fontelles_, Sep 06 2026
```

## A251335

OEIS entry: <https://oeis.org/A251335>  
Result obtained: **1 September 2026**  
Paper: [2592](../papers/2501-3000/2592-PROOF.pdf)

What the entry records as unsettled:

> Empirical for column k:

Proposed OEIS comment:

```
The empirical recurrences for columns k = 1 and 2 are true. ```
```

Proposed OEIS comment:

```

```

> Empirical for row n:


> Empirical for row n:

> Empirical for row n:
